# 21 — Feature phase (`BYOS`, and the later prefixes of roadmap-p27)

Waves 27 to 65. Waves 27 to 49 are the feature rows below (the audit rows start at "Wave 53" and carry the `SEC-AUD` findings of waves 53 to 65, the last being SEC-AUD-182). Plan, acceptance criteria, agent split and owner needs: [dev/roadmap-p27.md](../dev/roadmap-p27.md) (D-380) for waves 27 to 40, and [dev/roadmap-p28.md](../dev/roadmap-p28.md) for waves 41 to 49. Prefixes are those of roadmap-p27 section 4; each wave's integrator adds its items here.

Same conventions as [20-launch-readiness.md](20-launch-readiness.md): an item ticks only when its proof runs (a CI job, a Postgres or browser test, or a recorded run in the integration handoff), never when it is only written. The live Garage cases of `storage.yml` run only on CI; an item ticked on its mock and Postgres proofs says so, and its CI run is linked once the owner pushes.

## Wave 27 — BYOS core

Integration: [dev/handoff/integration-phase27.md](../dev/handoff/integration-phase27.md).

### BYOS-MNT — Mounts
- [x] **BYOS-MNT-001** [DB][BE] Mounts and sealed credentials: migration 1400 `storage_mounts`, `sr_storage::mounts` (`MountRepo`, `MountResolver`, sealed credentials), `CachedMountResolver`, user and guild mount CRUD routes, `sr-cli` rotate for mounts, `sr_storage::path::safe_key` _Proven 2026-10-26 on the merged tree (integration-phase27.md): `cargo test -p sr-storage`, `sr-db --test mounts_repo` (Postgres), `sr-api --lib` (mount route tests), `route_matrix`._

Waves 63 to 65 (D-643 to D-645): the test has a seventh row, `response_type` (`crates/sr-storage/src/probe.rs`), and a provider that ignores the response overrides fails it _Proven 2026-10-26 on the merged tree (integration-phase27.md): `sr-storage --test s3_probe_mock`, `sr-api --lib` (rate limit and route tests). The Garage live case runs on CI only._
- [x] **BYOS-MNT-003** [BE][CLI] CORS checker and apply: `.../cors/check`, `.../cors/apply`, daily job `storage.cors_recheck` (id 134), `sr-cli storage ensure-cors`. Integration: [dev/handoff/integration-phase30b.md](../dev/handoff/integration-phase30b.md).

- [x] **SEC-ENT-001** [FE][BUILD] The public build with one entry per surface (landing, auth, invite), the app build, `enterApp`, the CSP files, the legal renderer, the notices split, per-surface budgets (design: `docs/dev/entrance-isolation.md`) _Merged tree, 2026-10-30: `node scripts/build-web.mjs` (both builds; the `resolveId` guard now also works on Windows), the full web Vitest suite (355 files, 3114 tests, incl. router, entrance pages, `corePaths`, `enterApp`, `csp.test.ts`), the per-surface budgets (landing 113.6, auth 119.2, invite 111.4, app 236.7 KB gzip) and `check-entrance-isolation.mjs` with every surface ok._

Wave 50 (feat-p50-pwa.md): `e2e/static/pwa-security.spec.ts` exists and `web.yml` (`static-browser-checks`) runs it on the built dist; it now also covers the worker scope and the single `sr-static-<build>` cache, a public-only precache list, no `/app` request or cache entry from the public pages, the two manifests and the plain error pages (26 static tests green locally on the real two-build dist, Chromium). The `pwa-update.spec.ts` navigation races (reads and gotos cut short by the app's own 401 exit, a bare `reg.update()`) are fixed and the spec passed 4 of 4 repeats locally on the real dist. Proven in CI on main `a0b72d01` (wave 51, feat-p51-ci.md): Web run 42276293423, job `static-browser-checks` (128102255378) ran the 26 static tests green, all 12 of `pwa-security.spec.ts` including the five new ones; E2E run 42276293453, job `playwright (chromium)` (128093355269) passed the three `pwa-update.spec.ts` drills (build A to B with the purge, the anonymous update, the `PURGE_APP` sign-out; the spec is Chromium-only). The entrance isolation, smoke and journey specs do run on the built bundle in `e2e.yml` (three engines) and in `prod-smoke.yml`._
- [x] **SEC-ENT-003** [BE][OPS] The gate in `sr-server` (`[web] root`), the legal renderer, `Clear-Site-Data` on logout, the API oracle fixes, the Caddyfile and deployment files _Merged tree: `cargo test -p sr-server --test web_gate` (18), sr-api `route_matrix` and `openapi_names`, fmt and clippy `-D warnings` on sr-core, sr-api, sr-preflight and sr-server, `render-caddyfile.mjs --check`. Proven on CI at the merge of wave 37 (OPS-DOC-001, 2026-10-31): the ignored Postgres suite (`authz_matrix`, `immutable_rules`, `web_gate`) in [rust.yml run 41208735069](https://github.com/braxtonlayne/SIDEROOM/actions/runs/41208735069) and the stack through Caddy in [prod-smoke.yml run 41205318174](https://github.com/braxtonlayne/SIDEROOM/actions/runs/41205318174)._
- [ ] **SEC-ENT-004** [TEST][CI][DOCS] The per-surface checks (`scripts/check-entrance-isolation.mjs`, `e2e/tests/entrance-isolation.spec.ts`, `SR_E2E_WEB=server`, the dev and preview gates, the workflows, the docs) _Merged tree: `node --test "scripts/*.test.mjs"` (82 pass), the isolation check on the real dist (every surface ok), `publicDev()` wired into `vite.config.ts`, all 18 workflows parse, the deliberate leak fails the build guard and the check (integration-phase30b.md). Wave 50: `voice-soak.yml` now runs `check-entrance-isolation.mjs` on its dist and the anonymous and gate cases of `entrance-isolation.spec.ts` (Chromium) before the soak; all 18 workflows parse. Waits on: the owner's next voice-soak dispatch (`voice-soak.yml` runs only on manual dispatch and has not run since those steps were added; wave 51 found no other missing proof); the isolation check is in `web.yml` and `e2e.yml`, `entrance-isolation.spec.ts` runs on three engines in `e2e.yml` and through Caddy in `prod-smoke.yml`, and `repo-checks.yml` runs the script tests (p40 handoff keeps it unticked). Checked 2026-11-13 against waves 61 to 65: `check-entrance-isolation.mjs` and `entrance-isolation.spec.ts` were last changed by SEC-AUD-039, `voice-soak.yml` still runs both steps and is still dispatch-only, and no run of it is recorded since; 19 workflow files exist today. The staff gate of D-651 to D-653 is not part of this check: `scripts/check-staff-delivery.mjs` (run in `web.yml`, its tests in `repo-checks.yml`) and `sr-server --test web_gate` (`/app/staff/`) cover it._

## Wave 31 — The voice jukebox

Integration: [dev/handoff/integration-phase31.md](../dev/handoff/integration-phase31.md). Decisions: P31-JB-1 (owner:
keep the mic mono) and P31-JB-2 to P31-JB-5 (agent defaults the owner may change).

### MUS-JB — The voice jukebox
- [ ] **MUS-JB-001** [BE][FE] A stereo music track in E2EE voice: `video::Source::Music`, `VoiceCommand::SetMusic`, two audio mids per member in `sr-voice` (mono Opus 111 for the mic, stereo Opus 112 for music, `sr_voice_proto::sdp`), the per-listener mute hint, SPEAK required, `music_allowed` and the bitrate (migration 1560, read by `chat_repo`, `sr_music_allowed`/`sr_music_bitrate_kbps` on the channel object, sent to the SFU at a join and on edit), the gauge `sr_voice_music_egress_bits_per_second`; the web engine's `publishMusic` under the same frame encryptor (D-249), the listener's music mute and volume; `sr-sdk` and `packages/bot` send music _Merged tree: `sr-voice` (incl. `tests/music.rs`), `sr-voice-proto`, `sr-sdk` (default voice features, incl. the real-SFU bot DJ), `sr-gateway` (SetMusic at a join and on edit), `sr-chat` channel rules, `sr-db --test channel_music` (Postgres, migration 1560), vitest `src/voice` (engineMusic, streamMode), `packages/bot`, and Playwright `voice-live.spec.ts` on Chromium locally (the music step: B receives a second inbound audio stream, Opus, `channels: 2`; three passes). Waits on: the Firefox/WebKit live runs and the eight-member soak with music on (CI). Checked 2026-11-13: the music step of `voice-live.spec.ts` still runs on Chromium only (`e2e.yml`), and `voice-soak.spec.ts` has no music step. Waves 61 to 65 added per-track ingress caps in the SFU (D-631, D-639); a music track is capped at the room's `music_bitrate_kbps` x 1.5 + 64 kbit/s (`ingress::music_cap_kbps`, `crates/sr-voice/src/ingress.rs`), so a track at the 320 kbit/s ceiling still passes._ _(Wave 69, D-720: waits only for the owner's `voice-soak.yml` dispatch with `music` on (one dispatch serves RDY-VOX-002 and SEC-ENT-004 too); the step is built (SEC-AUD-234) and passed with two members. The Firefox and WebKit live runs are TV-HLS-005's download.)_ _(Wave 70: music survives an ICE restart and a full rejoin of the player in `voice-live.spec.ts` with two members (SEC-AUD-250); the soak dispatch, D-724, is still the owner's.)_
- [x] **MUS-JB-002** [BE][FE] Collaborative queue, vote-skip, synced local playback: migration 1570, `sr-chat` `jukebox/*`, `PgJukebox`, 13 routes under `/channels/{id}/jukebox`, `JUKEBOX_UPDATE`, the strip and the controller (Watch Party clock and drift policy), stream mode on the DJ's client (`musicSink.ts`: the element tapped into the music track) _Merged tree: `sr-chat --lib jukebox`, `sr-db --test jukebox_repo` (4, Postgres), `sr-api --lib` (presign scope), `route_matrix`, `openapi_names`, the authz matrix (Postgres, jukebox routes seeded), vitest `src/voice/jukebox` (incl. the DJ stream mode and the music sink), and Playwright `jukebox.spec.ts` on Chromium locally (two listeners within 250 ms after five seconds; three passes). ## Wave 45 — Crash telemetry and Showroom preview (roadmap P28)

### OPS — Crash telemetry
- [x] **OPS-CRASH-001** [BE+FE] Deterministic crash ring buffer and the `#crash-triage` feed (D-430 to D-434, threat-model row T45; migration 2160 `crash_reports`): the gated app keeps the last 60 deterministic ticks (Activity bridge commands as opaque enums, plus `sr-crash-tick` events for in-page game runtimes) and hooks unhandled errors, unhandled rejections, the router's and a top-level React error boundary, and WASM or WebGPU panics (a Rust panic without a panic hook is a `RuntimeError`, classified by type, name or `wasm://` frame); the report is BUILT from an allow-list (commit SHA, stack frames with a normalized file, line, column and function, tick inputs as enums, the route NAME, browser capability flags, timestamps) and nothing else is copied: no error message, no URL, query or host, id-like path pieces become `:id`; `POST /crash-reports` is session-only (D-385), capped at 32 KiB and rate limited per account and platform, and rebuilds the card from the same allow-list on the server, deduplicates by a SHA-256 of the commit and the top 5 normalized frames with a count, and the system user posts one structured JSON card per distinct crash in `#crash-triage` of the Staff Server (created idempotently, staff-only overwrites; repeats refresh the count in place at milestones); `GET /admin/crash-reports[/{id}]` gives staff and an agent run by one the card and replay buffer, and a replay read leaves the `crash.report.read` audit entry; route matrix and authz matrix rows; the shared hostile fixture `e2e/fixtures/crash-hostile.json` is fed to both allow-lists and to `scripts/check-crash-card.mjs`, the CI secret-pattern grep of a built card. **An agent proposes; a human merges:** nothing here deploys, merges or runs anything. See [handoff](../dev/handoff/feat-p45-crash-telemetry.md) _Proved: sr-api crash (12 incl. the hostile-payload test), sr-server `staff_feed_pg_tests::crash_triage_is_created_once_staff_only_and_receives_allow_listed_cards` (channel created once, staff-only overwrites, card posted with zero needles, dedupe and in-place refresh, early-start regression), route_matrix (6), authz_matrix (9), immutable_rules (25), cargo fmt --check and clippy -D warnings on sr-api, sr-db, sr-admin, sr-server, full vitest (442 files, 4036 tests; `src/crash` 18), tsc, eslint, prettier, i18n-audit, build-web, bundle budget (app chunk 247.9 KB gzip; report builder lazy), entrance isolation clean, `scripts/check-crash-card.test.mjs` (5), Playwright Chromium `crash-telemetry.spec.ts` (@gate, 2: synthetic WASM panic to `#crash-triage` allow-listed and deduplicated; body cap and rate limit)_ _Merged tree ([integration](../dev/handoff/integration-phase45.md)): `auth::policy` now refuses bots on `POST /crash-reports` as the route-matrix row says (route_matrix 6), the emulator's local presses feed the ring (`emu/__tests__/gamepad.test.ts`), `showroom/__tests__/showroom-crash.test.tsx` (a crash while the preview is on reports the route name and no fixture text; fixture clicks report nothing), sr-api lib 826, sr-server lib 253, authz_matrix 11, immutable_rules 25 plus the nightly matrix in normal, kill_features and kill_all, full vitest 444 files / 4051 tests, bundle budget (app chunk 248.2 KB gzip of the 250 KB single-chunk limit; `report-*.js` lazy), entrance isolation clean, Playwright Chromium `crash-telemetry.spec.ts` 2 pass on a fresh database with the server started before the Owner bootstrap_

## Wave 53 — Audit fixes (read-only audit of 2026-11-07)

### SEC-AUD — Security audit findings
- [x] **SEC-AUD-001** [BE/FE] Discord sign-in applies the same second-factor rule as password sign-in (TOTP or passkey, trusted devices honoured); `/login/2fa` offers the passkey for a Discord login. Handoff `fix-p53-auth.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-002** [BE/FE] Logout, logout-all, session end, password change or reset, the email-change revoke link and staff suspend, force-logout and reset-2FA publish `SessionsRevoked`; the gateway re-checks and closes revoked sockets (4004), with a 60 s re-check as a backstop; the web client signs out on 4004. Group-DM removal also drops the member from the DM call and its MLS group. Handoff `fix-p53-auth.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-003** [BE] A block in either direction closes a 1:1 DM to sends, reactions, poll votes, scheduled sends and calls (50007); group DMs are unchanged. Handoff `fix-p53-block.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-004** [BE] A released app keeps only its approved privileged intents while a new version is in review. Handoff `fix-p53-limits.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-005** [BE] Storage "companion" files are an allow-list (artwork, subtitles, shelf sidecars); other files need manage rights. Handoff `fix-p53-storage.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-006** [BE] Held messages and attachments survive channel and DM deletes (migration 2390 triggers; 400 `CHANNEL_UNDER_MODERATION_HOLD`); purge jobs lock and re-check holds before deleting objects; delisted mounts and single-object takedowns are refused on `/cdn/mount`; moving a taken-down file is refused; the ZIP central directory is checked before parsing; transcode redirects are followed only from the server itself. Handoff `fix-p53-storage.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-007** [BE] The HTTP rate limiter also charges unverified credentials to the client IP, and ids in bucket keys are normalised. Handoff `fix-p53-limits.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-008** [FE] The emulator starts with saving off when a save can't be read, instead of overwriting it; a refused (403) read counts as unreadable (D-560). Handoff `fix-p53-web.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-009** [BE] SFU per-member declarations and hints are capped, ICE-TCP connections are capped at 4096, and SetFlags before join keeps the token tier. Handoff `fix-p53-limits.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-010** [BE] `/gateway` caps messages at 256 KiB; unidentified sockets must IDENTIFY within 30 s, at most 32 per address, and count toward the connection cap. Handoff `fix-p53-auth.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-011** [BE] A blocked or ignored author's mentions don't badge or Web-Push; live presence isn't sent across a block. Handoff `fix-p53-block.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-012** [BE] Library comments, reviews and lists, reading presence and the profile's top friends and guestbook honour blocks and ignores. Handoff `fix-p53-block.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-013** [FE] Staff removal and invite revoke ask for the required reason (they always failed before). Handoff `fix-p53-web.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-014** [FE] A failed load never leaves an editable empty form (per-server profile, notes, birthday, Notes tool); a failed save never says "Saved". Handoff `fix-p53-web.md`.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-015** [FE] The embedded-app SDK validates state versions, scopes per-member keys to their writer, and rate-limits sync answers. Handoff `fix-p53-web.md`.

- [x] **SEC-AUD-081** [BE] `execute_branch_action` claims its request with a conditional UPDATE `approved` -> `executing` (a racing call answers Conflict); the finish needs `executing`; an error hands the claim back to `approved`. Migration 2590 widens the state CHECK.
- [x] **SEC-AUD-082** [BE] D-605: a staff account in a banned subtree goes to `review`, never the automatic freeze; the branch freeze and ban skip staff unless the caller is the Owner; the 30-day sweep never removes staff.

- [x] **SEC-AUD-085** [BE] An overturned appeal and its unban are ordered, not one database transaction: `decide_appeal_with` locks the open appeal, lifts the ban through an `AppealUnbanner` (a Person no longer banned is a no-op), then records the decision; an error leaves the ban in place.

- [x] **SEC-AUD-429** [TEST] In the e2e harness a skin's audio returns to 0 within 20 ms after any seek, on the base too (a direct `currentTime = 20` does it); isolate whether the harness (`page.route` tone) or the player resets it (found by the circuit and tidepool builders). Handoff `fix-p93-circuit.md`. Fixed in wave 94 (D-935): the cause was the player, not the harness. Every seek and every stall fires `waiting`, which set `loading`, which `onStore` read as a new load and restarted the track at 0. A `loading` reported through `waiting` is now flagged and never enters `load()`; a track switched during a stall loads at once; store-driven loads and the gapless hand-over are unchanged. Five engine tests and the seek spec prove it (min at or above the target in every case); a real server file was not run in wave 100 (D-954): the local stack stores in memory, so a member's upload (chat attachment or music-library track) has no browser-usable URL (`memory://`), and a real run needs an S3-compatible bucket. The server's `/cdn/{*path}` handler answers a single byte range with 206 (read, not run). Handoff `fix-p100-seek.md`. Handoffs `fix-p94-seek.md`, `fix-p94-seekfix.md`.

A 403 AccessDenied from `GetBucketCors` (what R2 may answer to an Object Read and Write token scoped to one bucket) becomes a 409 "The service refused to read or write the bucket's CORS rules" (`crates/sr-api/src/routes/storage/mount_ops.rs`), `cors_ok_at` stays empty and every upload and presign answers `CORS_NOT_READY` though the bucket's CORS is right. D-1032: fall back to the live preflight on a 403 too. Found by r2. Wave 110 (one Rust builder). Handoff `fix-p109-r2.md`. Closed in wave 110 (D-1032; mounts): `check` falls back to the live preflight when the config read is refused (HTTP 403, or the S3 code `AccessDenied` or `AccessDeniedException`, judged by status and code, never by message text); only a preflight that answers with the allow-origin header sets `cors_ok_at`, and a refused, failed or unrunnable preflight leaves today's 409. Tests in `crates/sr-storage/tests/cors_denied_fallback.rs` (5, passing) and `s3_probe_mock` (20). Not tested over HTTP (the in-process fake S3 of `mounts_tests.rs` cannot set response headers). Handoff `fix-p110-mounts.md`.
